问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 是 LP87702-Q1；VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：U1700的每个引脚连接的网络如下（基于原理图文件825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF中的引脚映射）： - nINT → MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1 - EN1 → MCU_5V_EN - VIN_B0_1 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_1 → VCC3V3 - VANA → VCC3V3 - SW_BST → N19935658 - NRST → N19935946 - SDA(EN3) → MCU_SDA_P15.5 - SCL(EN2) → MCU_SCL_P15.4 - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - CLKIN(GPO2) → MCU_WD_INH - WDI → MCU_WDI_P13.3 - VOUT_BST → VCC50 - PG1(GPO1) → N19936014 - WD_RESET → TC367_RESET_N - SW_B1_1 → N19935668 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - SW_B0_1 → N19935443 - SW_B0_2 → N19935443 - VIN_B0_2 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_2 → VCC3V3 - SW_B1_2 → N19935668 - PAD → GND - AGND → GND - PGND_BST → GND - PGND_B1_2 → GND - PGND_B1_1 → GND - PGND_B0_2 → GND - PGND_B0_1 → GND **证据来源**：原理图文件825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF中U1700的引脚映射记录（circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1700）。该记录完整列出了每个引脚与其连接的网络名称，无缺失项。
评分正文：U1700的每个引脚连接的网络如下（基于原理图文件825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF中的引脚映射）： - nINT → MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1 - EN1 → MCU_5V_EN - VIN_B0_1 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_1 → VCC3V3 - VANA → VCC3V3 - SW_BST → N19935658 - NRST → N19935946 - SDA(EN3) → MCU_SDA_P15.5 - SCL(EN2) → MCU_SCL_P15.4 - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - CLKIN(GPO2) → MCU_WD_INH - WDI → MCU_WDI_P13.3 - VOUT_BST → VCC50 - PG1(GPO1) → N19936014 - WD_RESET → TC367_RESET_N - SW_B1_1 → N19935668 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - SW_B0_1 → N19935443 - SW_B0_2 → N19935443 - VIN_B0_2 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_2 → VCC3V3 - SW_B1_2 → N19935668 - PAD → GND - AGND → GND - PGND_BST → GND - PGND_B1_2 → GND - PGND_B1_1 → GND - PGND_B0_2 → GND - PGND_B0_1 → GND **证据来源**：原理图文件825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF中U1700的引脚映射记录（circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1700）。该记录完整列出了每个引脚与其连接的网络名称，无缺失项。
检索上下文：Pin mapping for U1700: nINT -> MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1, EN1 -> MCU_5V_EN, VIN_B0_1 -> VCC3V3, VIN_B1_1 -> VCC3V3, VANA -> VCC3V3, SW_BST -> N19935658, NRST -> N19935946, SDA(EN3) -> MCU_SDA_P15.5, SCL(EN2) -> MCU_SCL_P15.4, FB_B1 -> VCC1V8, CLKIN(GPO2) -> MCU_WD_INH, WDI -> MCU_WDI_P13.3, VOUT_BST -> VCC50, PG1(GPO1) -> N19936014, WD_RESET -> TC367_RESET_N, SW_B1_1 -> N19935668, FB_B0 -> VCC1V1, SW_B0_1 -> N19935443, SW_B0_2 -> N19935443, VIN_B0_2 -> VCC3V3, VIN_B1_2 -> VCC3V3, SW_B1_2 -> N19935668, PAD -> GND, AGND -> GND, PGND_BST -> GND, PGND_B1_2 -> GND, PGND_B1_1 -> GND, PGND_B0_2 -> GND, PGND_B0_1 -> GND. | Instance U1700: LP87702-Q1, 20200424007, LP87702RRHBRQ1, pqfn50p5x5x90-32.

问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：直接结论：网络 VCC3V3 上连接的主要芯片有 U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804。 支撑证据：证据 [1]（电路网表 Net VCC3V3）显示该网络直接连接以下芯片引脚：U1500.VIN、U1600.VCCIN、U1700.VIN_B0_1、U1700.VIN_B1_1、U1700.VANA、U1700.VIN_B0_2、U1700.VIN_B1_2、U1800.VIN（两个引脚）、U1800.MODE/SYNC、U1802.VIN、U1804.VIN（两个引脚）。上述引脚所属的芯片即为所连接的六颗主要芯片（U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804）。其他连接对象（如电容、电阻、电感、测试点、X2000晶振/连接器）不属于主要芯片，故未列出。
评分正文：直接结论：网络 VCC3V3 上连接的主要芯片有 U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804。 支撑证据：证据 [1]（电路网表 Net VCC3V3）显示该网络直接连接以下芯片引脚：U1500.VIN、U1600.VCCIN、U1700.VIN_B0_1、U1700.VIN_B1_1、U1700.VANA、U1700.VIN_B0_2、U1700.VIN_B1_2、U1800.VIN（两个引脚）、U1800.MODE/SYNC、U1802.VIN、U1804.VIN（两个引脚）。上述引脚所属的芯片即为所连接的六颗主要芯片（U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804）。其他连接对象（如电容、电阻、电感、测试点、X2000晶振/连接器）不属于主要芯片，故未列出。

问题：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含哪些模块电路？
参考答案：HSI 2.3 节列出 KL30 电源管理、唤醒、电源、TC377 最小系统、EQ6 最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗、温度采样、系统内电源电压采样、ETH、CAN/CAN FD 和 3V3 过压保护等电路。
实际回答：**直接结论：** ADAS 项目软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构的模块电路（Module circuit）共包含以下 12 个电路模块： 1. KL30 电源管理电路（KL30 supply management circuit） 2. 唤醒电路（Wake Up circuit） 3. 电源电路（power circuit） 4. TC377 最小系统（TC377 minimum system） 5. EQ6 最小系统（EQ6 minimum system） 6. 摄像头最小系统（camera minimum system） 7. 看门狗电路（Watchdog circuit） 8. 温度采样电路（Temp Sample circuit） 9. 系统内电源电压采样电源（The power System Voltage Sampling circuit） 10. ETH 电路（ETH circuit） 11. CAN/CAN FD 电路（CAN/CAN FD circuit） 12. 3V3 过压保护电路（3V3 overvoltage protection circuit） **支撑证据：** - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 的目录中明确列出上述 12 个模块电路（证据[3] 第 2.3 节）。 - 同一文档的后续内容直接列出了这些模块的名称（证据[4] 中依次列出“KL30电源管理电路”、“唤醒电路”、“电源电路”……至“3V3过压保护电路”）。 **缺失信息：** 无。证据完全覆盖该问题，未发现缺失项。
评分正文：**直接结论：** ADAS 项目软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构的模块电路（Module circuit）共包含以下 12 个电路模块： 1. KL30 电源管理电路（KL30 supply management circuit） 2. 唤醒电路（Wake Up circuit） 3. 电源电路（power circuit） 4. ETH 电路（ETH circuit） 11. CAN/CAN FD 电路（CAN/CAN FD circuit） 12. 3V3 过压保护电路（3V3 overvoltage protection circuit） **支撑证据：** - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 的目录中明确列出上述 12 个模块电路（证据[3] 第 2.3 节）。 - 同一文档的后续内容直接列出了这些模块的名称（证据[4] 中依次列出“KL30电源管理电路”、“唤醒电路”、“电源电路”……至“3V3过压保护电路”）。 **缺失信息：** 无。证据完全覆盖该问题，未发现缺失项。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F06/F11</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS正常工作电压范围9~16VDC，系统需求需要对产品供电KL30进行电源管理，硬件电路对UBD电源网络进行ADC采样。 ADAS normal operating voltage range is 9~16VDC. System requirements require power management for the product power supply KL30. The hardware circuit performs ADC sampling on the UBD power supply.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号是由MCU I/O控制的数字信号，用于使能KL30电压采样功能。 MCU_UB_SW_P00.10 signal is a digital signal controlled by MCU I/O, used to enable KL30 voltage sampling function. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. MCU的ADC采集到UBD后进行电源管理，当软件诊断到UBD电压范围异常时，MCU会关断EQ和其他负载工作电源，确保ADAS功能安全。 The ADC of MCU collects UBD for power management. When the software detects that UBD voltage range is abnormal, the MCU will turn off EQ and other load power supplies to ensure the functional safety of ADAS.</td></tr></table> | When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. 评分上下文：8/8 段，12000/12288 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-joint-main-pmicsuccess0.3350.3580.750not_applicable1.0000.5000.5000.714not_applicable
问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：**直接结论：** 1. **主PMIC型号**：给板子供电的主PMIC是 **LP87702**。 2. **输出电压**：该PMIC输出三路电压：**VCC1V8**、**VCC1V1** 和 **VCC50**。 3. **软件配置**：软件通过MCU的GPIO和I2C接口进行配置。具体为：使用 **MCU的P15.3引脚**（GPIO）使能VCC50输出；使用 **MCU_SCL_P15.4** 和 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** 引脚（I2C总线，I2C地址0x08）来设置VCC1V8和VCC1V1的输出电压及使能；使用 **MCU的P12.1引脚**（GPIO）复位该芯片。 **支撑证据：** 1. **主PMIC型号及输出电压**： * 文档[9]中明确说明：“VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” * 文档[10]中进一步确认：“采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 2. **软件配置方式**： * 文档[10]详细描述了配置方式：“用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1输出电压和使能。用MCU的P12.1引脚复位该芯片。” * 文档[14]提供了I2C地址信息：“外挂LP87702和TPS62872 ... LP87702地址：0x08”，并确认了MCU_SDA_P15.5和MCU_SCL_P15.4用于I2C通信。 * 电路连接证据[6]和[7]显示，Group 17 (Power/PMIC) 与 Group 18 (Power/PMIC) 通过 `MCU_SCL_P15.4` 和 `MCU_SDA_P15.5` 网络连接，这与上述文档中的I2C通信描述一致。 * 电路连接证据[1]显示 `MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1` 网络连接至U1700（LP87702）的 `nINT` 引脚，这与文档[14]中描述的“LP87702的输出中断信号”功能相符，但该引脚在软件配置中作为复位功能使用，可能由硬件设计决定。 **缺失信息：** 无。
评分正文：**直接结论：** 1. **主PMIC型号**：给板子供电的主PMIC是 **LP87702**。 2. **输出电压**：该PMIC输出三路电压：**VCC1V8**、**VCC1V1** 和 **VCC50**。 3. **软件配置**：软件通过MCU的GPIO和I2C接口进行配置。具体为：使用 **MCU的P15.3引脚**（GPIO）使能VCC50输出；使用 **MCU_SCL_P15.4** 和 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** 引脚（I2C总线，I2C地址0x08）来设置VCC1V8和VCC1V1的输出电压及使能；使用 **MCU的P12.1引脚**（GPIO）复位该芯片。 **支撑证据：** 1. **主PMIC型号及输出电压**： * 文档[9]中明确说明：“VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” * 文档[10]中进一步确认：“采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 2. **软件配置方式**： * 文档[10]详细描述了配置方式：“用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1输出电压和使能。用MCU的P12.1引脚复位该芯片。” * 文档[14]提供了I2C地址信息：“外挂LP87702和TPS62872 ... LP87702地址：0x08”，并确认了MCU_SDA_P15.5和MCU_SCL_P15.4用于I2C通信。 * 电路连接证据[6]和[7]显示，Group 17 (Power/PMIC) 与 Group 18 (Power/PMIC) 通过 `MCU_SCL_P15.4` 和 `MCU_SDA_P15.5` 网络连接，这与上述文档中的I2C通信描述一致。 * 电路连接证据[1]显示 `MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1` 网络连接至U1700（LP87702）的 `nINT` 引脚，这与文档[14]中描述的“LP87702的输出中断信号”功能相符，但该引脚在软件配置中作为复位功能使用，可能由硬件设计决定。 **缺失信息：** 无。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. Power on When the wake-up signal becomes active, the system's primary power supply outputs the VCC3V3 voltage, and the MCU starts operating. The MCU_UB_SW_P00.10 signal then outputs a logic high level to enable the KL30 voltage sampling circuit. Subsequently, UBSW_ACQ outputs a divided analog signal, after which the MCU begins ADC sampling of the UBSW_ACQ signal.运行模式 MCU实时采样UBSW_ACQ用于电源管理功能。 2.Run mode MCU real-time sampling UBSW_ACQ is used for power management functions. 下电模式 唤醒信号无效后，MCU在off delay期间将MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号配置为逻辑低，关闭KL30采样功能，系统下电。 3. Power off After the wake-up signal is invalid, the MCU will configure the MCU_UB_Sw_P00.10 signal to logic low during offdelay, turn off the KL30 sampling function, and power down the system. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td> 参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirements specification. 问题：0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：## 直接结论 - **0.75V 电源产生芯片**：TI TPS62872。 - **输入**：VCC3V3（3.3V 电源轨）。 - **文档配置要求**： - 项目设计文档（HSI 模块方案）规定：通过 MCU 的 P22.0 引脚使能芯片，通过 I2C 接口（MCU_SDA_P15.5、MCU_SCL_P15.4）设置输出电压和通信。 - 芯片数据手册（TPS6287x-Q1）提供了详细的外部元件选型指南，包括电感推荐值（典型 110 nH）、输出电容（最小 203 μF，推荐 X7R 陶瓷电容）、补偿网络（电阻、电容）、开关频率设置（通过 FSEL 引脚电阻选择）等，但项目文档中未指定具体数值。 --- ## 支撑证据 ### 1. 产生芯片和输入 - 文档 [10]（0eb6659a764f4d29）：“VCC3V3 转 VCC0V75 应用于 EQ6 的内核供电……现选型 TI 的方案 TPS62872。” - 文档 [12]（b6943ae371d76077）：“VCC3V3 转 VCC0V75 应用于 EQ6 的内核供电……电源方案 TPS62872。” - 两文档均明确输入为 **VCC3V3**（3.3V 电源轨），该电源轨由 KL30 转 VCC3V3 的一级 DC-DC 产生（文档 [12] 模块方案描述）。 ### 2. 配置要求（项目文档） - 文档 [10]：“用 MCU 的 P22.0 引脚使能芯片，用 MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4 引脚 I2C 通信来设置输出电压和通信。” - 文档 [12] 重复同样的使能和 I2C 配置描述。 - 项目文档中未列出外部电阻、电容、电感的具体数值，仅说明通过 I2C 编程完成输出电压设定。 ### 3. 配置要求（芯片数据手册） - 文档 [14]（af98fef66e3629af）：“TI 推荐电感起始值 110 nH……开关频率可通过 FSEL 引脚电阻选择为 1.5 MHz、2.25 MHz、2.5 MHz 或 3.0 MHz。” - 文档 [15]（136b2d26099fb0e7）：“输出电容最小值为 203 μF……推荐低 ESR 多层陶瓷电容（X7R 介质）。” - 文档 [17]（d6a0c0ee0360415f）和 [19]（2d98070e9d1ea13a）提供了补偿电容、动态电压调整等详细配置方法。 - **注意**：数据手册内容属于通用设计指南，项目文档未直接引用这些具体数值，因此实际配置需结合数据手册和系统要求。 --- ## 缺失信息 - 项目文档中未给出 **外部电阻、电容、电感的具体数值**，也未明确开关频率、输出电压的精确 I2C 寄存器设置值。 - 数据手册中的配置计算（如电感值、电容值、补偿电阻等）需根据具体负载和瞬态要求确定，但项目文档未提供 TI 推荐值以外的定制化参数。

The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. This register returns the device status flags. 10 Application and Implementation Note Information in the following applications sections is not part of the Tl component specification, and Tl does not warrant its accuracy or completeness. Tl's customers are responsible for determining suitability of components for their purposes, as well as validating and testing their design implementation to confirm systemfunctionality. 10.1 Application Information The following section discusses selection of the external components to complete the power supply design for typical a application. 10.2 Typical Application 10.2.1 Design Requirements Preliminary Calculations The maximum allowable deviation of the power supply is ±3.3%. The DC accuracy of the TPS6287x-Q1 is specified as ±1%, therefore, the maximum output voltage variation during a transient is given by: VoUT = ± VouT × (3.3% - 1%) = ±17.25 mV 10.2.2 Detailed Design Procedure The following subsections describe how to calculate the external components required to meet the specified transient requirements of a given application. The calculations include the worst-case variation of components and use the RMS method to combine the variation of uncorrelated parameters. 10.2.2.1 Selecting the Inductor The TPS6287x-Q1 devices have been optimized for inductors in the range 50 nH to 300 nH. If the transient response of the converter is limited by the slew rate of the current in the inductor, using a smaller inductor can improve performance. However, the output ripple current increases as the value of the inductor decreases and higher output current ripple generates higher output voltage ripple, which adds to the transient overshoot or undershoot. The optimal configuration for a given application is always a trade-off between a number of parameters. Tl recommends a starting value of 110 nH for typical applications. | Rounding up, the closest standard value from the E24 series is 2.4 kQ. 10.2.2.4Selecting the Output Capacitors In practice, the total output capacitance is typically comprised of a combination of different capacitors, in which larger capacitors provide the load current at lower frequencies and smaller capacitors provide the load current at higher frequencies. The value, type, and location of the output capacitors are critical for correct operation. TI recommends low-ESR multilayer ceramic capacitors with an X7R dielectric (or similar) for best performance. The TPS6287x-Q1 devices feature a butterfly layout with two GND pins on opposite sides of the package. This allows the output capacitors to be placed symmetrically on the PCB such that the electromagnetic fields generated cancel each other out, thereby reducing EMI. The transient response of the converter is limited by one of two criteria: · The slew rate of the current through the inductor, in which case, the feedback loop of the converter saturates. · The maximum allowed ratio of converter bandwidth to switching frequency, in which the converter remains in regulation (that is, its loop does not saturate). Tl recommends a minimum ratio of four for typical applications. Which of the above criteria applies in any given application depends on the operating conditions and component values used. Therefore, Tl recommends that the user calculate the output capacitance for both cases, and select the higher of the two values. If the converter remains in regulation, the minimum output required capacitance is given by: If the converter loop saturates, the minimum output capacitance is given by: In this case, choose Cout(min) = 203 μF as the larger of the two values for the output capacitance. When calculating worst-case component values, use the value calculated above as the minimum output capacitance required. For ceramic capacitors, the maximum capacitance when considering tolerance, DC bias, temperature, and aging effects is typically two times the minimum capacitance. In this case, the maximum capacitance CouT(max) is 406 μF. 10.2.2.5 Selecting the Compensation Capacitor, Cc First, use Equation 17 to calculate the bandwidth of the inner loop: BWINNER = 2π × N x CoUT(max) 12.5 × 10-6 BWINNER = = 89 kHz 2πt × 110 × 10× 203.2 × 10-6 1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F32</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>3V3转1V25_MCU用于MCU的核供电 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is used for the core power supply of MCU</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>该BUCK电源使用MCU的内置控制器进行控制，外部需要选型功率开关管和电感电容。 The BUCK power supply is controlled by the built-in controller of the MCU, and the external selection of power switches and inductors and capacitors is required.</td></tr></table> | Next, calculate the product of gmRz: If gmRz > than 1, use Equation 20 to calculate the recommended value of Cc. The value of this capacitor is not critical; 10-pF or 22-pF capacitors are suitable for typical applications. | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | During start-up, the output voltage ramps up to the target value set by the VSEL pin before ramping up or down to any new value programmed to the device over the I2C interface. 9.3.6.3Non-DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoint If none of the default voltage range or voltage setpoint combinations are suitable for the application, the user can change these device settings through I2C before the user enables the device. Then, when the user pulls the EN pin high, the device starts up with the desired start-up voltage. Note that if the user changes the device settings through I?C while the device is ramping, the device ignores the changes until the ramp is complete 9.3.6.4 Dynamic Voltage Scaling If the user changes the output voltage setpoint while the DC/DC converter is operating, the device ramps up or down to the new voltage setting in a controlled way. The VRAMP[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register sets the slew rate when the device ramps from one voltage to another during DVS (see Table 9-6). Note that ramping the output to a higher voltage requires additional output current, so that during DvS, the converter must generate a total output current given by: dVour lour = lour c) + Cour dVOouT where · lout is the total current the converter must generate while ramping to a higher voltage. louT(Dc) is the DC load current.· Cout is the total output capacitance. dVout/dt is the slew rate of the output voltage (programmable in the range 0.5 mV/μs to 10 mV/us). For correct operation, ensure that the total output current during DVS does not exceed the current limit of the device. 9.3.7 Compensation (CoMP) The CoMP pin is the connection point for an external compensation network. A series-connected resistor and capacitor to GOSNS is sufficient for typical applications. The series-connected resistor also provides enough scope to optimize the loop response for a wide range of operating conditions. When using multiple devices in a stacked configuration, all devices share a common compensation network, and the COMP pin makes sure there is equal current sharing between them (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.8 ModeSelection and Clock Synchronization (MODE/SYNC) A high level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects forced PWM operation. A low level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects power save operation, in which, the device automatically transitions between PWM and PFM, according to the load conditions. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. 问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节声明存在 3V3 过压保护电路；EDF 只能用于说明观察到的二极管或 TVS 连接拓扑，不能在没有数据手册证据时断言具体阈值或保护能力。
实际回答：是的，3.3V电源（VCC3V3_MCU）有硬件外部过压保护电路，其实现方式是一个基于电压监测和MOSFET开关的电源切断电路，触发阈值为4.03V。 **支撑证据：** 1. **存在性与目的**：描述模块F18的文档明确指出，AOU要求MCU增加外部过压保护电路用于监控3V3_MCU电压，该模块功能定义即为“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”[11][6]。 2. **实现电路与工作原理**：文档[6]的模块方案详细描述了其工作原理，核心是使用一个电压监测器件和两个MOSFET（一个NMOS, 一个PMOS）： * **正常状态**：当VCC3V3电压大于MOSFET的开启阈值（VGS门限）但小于4.03V时，NMOS和PMOS均导通，VCC3V3_SW与VCC3V3直通，为后级供电。 * **过压保护状态**：当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，监测器件的cathode引脚输出逻辑低电平，导致NMOS和PMOS关闭，VCC3V3_SW断电，从而对后级负载起到过压保护作用。 * **低电压状态**：当VCC3V3电压低于MOSFET的开启阈值时，NMOS和PMOS也处于关闭状态，VCC3V3_SW无电。 **缺失信息：** 无。
评分正文：是的，3.3V电源（VCC3V3_MCU）有硬件外部过压保护电路，其实现方式是一个基于电压监测和MOSFET开关的电源切断电路，触发阈值为4.03V。 **支撑证据：** 1. **存在性与目的**：描述模块F18的文档明确指出，AOU要求MCU增加外部过压保护电路用于监控3V3_MCU电压，该模块功能定义即为“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”[11][6]。 2. **实现电路与工作原理**：文档[6]的模块方案详细描述了其工作原理，核心是使用一个电压监测器件和两个MOSFET（一个NMOS, 一个PMOS）： * **正常状态**：当VCC3V3电压大于MOSFET的开启阈值（VGS门限）但小于4.03V时，NMOS和PMOS均导通，VCC3V3_SW与VCC3V3直通，为后级供电。 * **过压保护状态**：当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，监测器件的cathode引脚输出逻辑低电平，导致NMOS和PMOS关闭，VCC3V3_SW断电，从而对后级负载起到过压保护作用。 * **低电压状态**：当VCC3V3电压低于MOSFET的开启阈值时，NMOS和PMOS也处于关闭状态，VCC3V3_SW无电。 **缺失信息：** 无。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. 问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：板上共有 **3 个 TPS22918 电源开关**，实例编号分别为 **U1803**、**U1802** 和 **U1500**。每个实例的输入、输出和使能引脚连接如下： ### 1. U1803 - **输入**：VCC1V8（来自电源转换芯片的1.8V输出） - **输出**：VCC1V8_EQ（用于EQ6系统供电） - **使能**：MCU_1V8_EQ_EN（由MCU的P20.0引脚控制） - **证据**： - 实例U1803被识别为TPS22918（证据[9]）。 - 文档[13]描述：“采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ，给EQ6供电。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” - 网络MCU_1V8_EQ_EN连接U1803.ON（证据[6]）。 ### 2. U1802 - **输入**：VCC3V3（系统3.3V电源） - **输出**：VCC3V3_EQ（用于EQ6 IO和摄像头供电） - **使能**：MCU_3V3_EQ_EN（由MCU的P20.3引脚控制） - **证据**： - 实例U1802被识别为TPS22918（证据[12]）。 - 文档[15]描述：“采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_EQ，提供给EQ6和摄像头供电。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。” - 网络MCU_3V3_EQ_EN连接U1802.ON（证据[7]）。 ### 3. U1500 - **输入**：VCC3V3（系统3.3V电源） - **输出**：VCC3V3_ETH（用于MCU的ETH供电） - **使能**：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN（由MCU的P21.0引脚控制） - **证据**： - 实例U1500被识别为TPS22918（证据[10]）。 - 文档[16]描述：“采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_ETH，提供给MCU的ETH供电。P21.0引脚使能该芯片。” - 网络MCU_3V3_ETH_EN连接U1500.ON（证据[8]）。 **注意**：以上输入和输出网络名称（如VCC1V8、VCC3V3_EQ等）来源于文字文档描述，电路图原理图中未直接标注这些网络名称，但文档描述与电路图实例对应关系一致，无证据冲突。

问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：**直接结论：** 1. **以太网PHY的电源要求：** 以太网PHY需要两个电压轨：**1.0V**（对应网络VCC1V0_ETH）和 **3.3V**（对应网络VCC3V3_ETH）。 2. **1.0V电源轨的供电链路：** 1.0V电源轨（VCC1V0_ETH）来源于一个LDO（U1501），该LDO的输入电源是3.3V电源轨（VCC3V3_ETH）。**（缺口：从系统输入电源到VCC3V3_ETH的完整供电链路未找到直接证据。）** **支撑证据：** * **关于以太网PHY的电源要求：** * 电路图显示，以太网PHY芯片（U1400）的引脚连接到了网络VCC1V0_ETH和VCC3V3_ETH[10]。 * 系统设计文档明确列出了以太网PHY的电源监控要求，包括VCC3V3_ETH和VCC1V0_ETH，并给出了VCC1V0_ETH的精度要求为0.9V～1.1V[2][5]。 * **关于1.0V电源轨的供电链路：** * 电路图显示，LDO芯片（U1501）的输入（IN）和使能（EN）引脚连接至网络VCC3V3_ETH，其输出（OUT）引脚连接至网络VCC1V0_ETH[11]。这表明VCC1V0_ETH是由VCC3V3_ETH通过LDO U1501直接转换而来。 **缺失信息：** * **从系统输入电源到VCC3V3_ETH的完整供电链路：** 现有证据[8][9]仅描述了系统输入电源（如12V电池）通过一级DC-DC（如LN10046）转换为3.3V，但未明确该3.3V电源轨是否就是VCC3V3_ETH或其上游电源。因此，从系统输入到VCC3V3_ETH的完整转换链路、中间转换器型号及具体连接关系均无直接证据。
评分正文：**直接结论：** 1. **以太网PHY的电源要求：** 以太网PHY需要两个电压轨：**1.0V**（对应网络VCC1V0_ETH）和 **3.3V**（对应网络VCC3V3_ETH）。 2. **1.0V电源轨的供电链路：** 1.0V电源轨（VCC1V0_ETH）来源于一个LDO（U1501），该LDO的输入电源是3.3V电源轨（VCC3V3_ETH）。**（缺口：从系统输入电源到VCC3V3_ETH的完整供电链路未找到直接证据。）** **支撑证据：** * **关于以太网PHY的电源要求：** * 电路图显示，以太网PHY芯片（U1400）的引脚连接到了网络VCC1V0_ETH和VCC3V3_ETH[10]。 * 系统设计文档明确列出了以太网PHY的电源监控要求，包括VCC3V3_ETH和VCC1V0_ETH，并给出了VCC1V0_ETH的精度要求为0.9V～1.1V[2][5]。 * **关于1.0V电源轨的供电链路：** * 电路图显示，LDO芯片（U1501）的输入（IN）和使能（EN）引脚连接至网络VCC3V3_ETH，其输出（OUT）引脚连接至网络VCC1V0_ETH[11]。这表明VCC1V0_ETH是由VCC3V3_ETH通过LDO U1501直接转换而来。 **缺失信息：** * **从系统输入电源到VCC3V3_ETH的完整供电链路：** 现有证据[8][9]仅描述了系统输入电源（如12V电池）通过一级DC-DC（如LN10046）转换为3.3V，但未明确该3.3V电源轨是否就是VCC3V3_ETH或其上游电源。因此，从系统输入到VCC3V3_ETH的完整转换链路、中间转换器型号及具体连接关系均无直接证据。

仅当PHY为MASTER且配置成管理模式时，上电自动进入待机模式。如果是自动模式，上电后自动进入正常模式。 4、 断电模式 当任何一个电源轨低于POR阈值（0.6V）时，PHY处于断电状态，所有数字IO都将保持高阻态。 休眠模式 当有休眠指令且MDI总线空闲时，将进入休眠模式，除了能量检测外，禁用所有PHY模块。在睡眠模式下，所有寄存器配置都会丢失。 Normal mode Power on，Normal Run mode, Ethernet transceiver is normal. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | None：MCU采样摄像头的1V8电源电压，精度要求：1.7~1.9V; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：U6; None：AN14; None：AN14/EVADC_G1CH6/EDSADC_EDS3PB; None：ADC，分辨率10bit; None：VCC1V8_CAM_ACQ; None：/; None：I; None：索尼摄像头模组电源1.8V电压采样。 U1V8_CAM_ACQ=3.3*ADC值/1024 精度：1.7~1.9V 硬件滤波截止频率：≥121.7Hz 硬件滤波时间：≤1.5ms; None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：Config; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：H19; None：P20.2; None：P20.2/TESTMODE; None：TESTMODE; None：—; None：/; None：I; None：TESTMODE： 高：正常工作模式 低：英飞凌内部测试模式; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 None：Config; MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：B; None：A14; None：P14.5; None：P14.5/GTM_TIM1_IN0_4/GTM_TIM0_IN0_4/PMS_HWCFG1IN/GTM_DTMA2_0/GTM_TOUT85/ERAY0_TXD; None：PMS_HWCFG1IN; None：—; None：/; None：I; None：EVR33控制内部3.3V电压转换: 高：内部使用5V转换为3.3V电压 低：禁止5V转换为3.3V电压; None：HI-Z，硬件外部下拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. 问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：主控MCU的型号是 **SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**。 * 证据10（文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）明确说明：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”。 * 证据14（同一文档的模块列表）中，F01模块（MCU）的功能定义列为“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。
评分正文：主控MCU的型号是 **SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**。 * 证据10（文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）明确说明：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”。 * 证据14（同一文档的模块列表）中，F01模块（MCU）的功能定义列为“SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。

OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | Sheet名称：0_Instruction Manual; 内容摘要：使用说明; 问题：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是谁？说清楚输入、三路输出和软件配置出处。
参考答案：U1700 LP87702-Q1 从 VCC3V3 取电，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50，软件配置出处为 HSI 7.2 节。
实际回答：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是 **LP87702**。 **1. 型号与输入** - **结论**：电源管理芯片型号为 LP87702，其输入电压为 **VCC3V3**（3.3V）。 - **证据**：文档[19]明确说明：“采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 **2. 三路输出规格** - **结论**：LP87702 的三路输出分别为 **VCC1V8**、**VCC1V1** 和 **VCC50**，用于给系统不同部分供电。 - **证据**：文档[19]指出这三路输出分别用于为EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH等供电（VCC1V8），为EQ6、DDR供电（VCC1V1），以及为CAN收发器供电（VCC50）。 - **缺失信息**：证据中未提供这三路输出的具体电流规格（如最大电流值）。 **3. 软件配置出处** - **结论**：LP87702 的软件配置（寄存器设置）在文档 **“LP87702 Configuration”** 章节中，该章节包含了运行期间需要通过I2C配置的寄存器列表。 - **证据**：文档[3]中明确列出了“LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”章节，并说明“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”。此外，文档[20]提到看门狗喂狗参数由LP87702的OTP文件配置。
评分正文：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是 **LP87702**。 **1. 型号与输入** - **结论**：电源管理芯片型号为 LP87702，其输入电压为 **VCC3V3**（3.3V）。 - **证据**：文档[19]明确说明：“采用LP87702芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50”。 **2. 三路输出规格** - **结论**：LP87702 的三路输出分别为 **VCC1V8**、**VCC1V1** 和 **VCC50**，用于给系统不同部分供电。 - **证据**：文档[19]指出这三路输出分别用于为EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH等供电（VCC1V8），为EQ6、DDR供电（VCC1V1），以及为CAN收发器供电（VCC50）。 - **缺失信息**：证据中未提供这三路输出的具体电流规格（如最大电流值）。 **3. 软件配置出处** - **结论**：LP87702 的软件配置（寄存器设置）在文档 **“LP87702 Configuration”** 章节中，该章节包含了运行期间需要通过I2C配置的寄存器列表。 - **证据**：文档[3]中明确列出了“LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”章节，并说明“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”。此外，文档[20]提到看门狗喂狗参数由LP87702的OTP文件配置。

MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F17</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>上电模式、下电模式、运行模式 Power on mode, Power off mode, Run mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 上电模式 MCU通过将MCU_3V3_ETH_EN信号配置为逻辑高使能VCC3V3_ETH电源输出，用于MCU ETH系统供电。 2、 下电模式 MCU通过将MCU_3V3_ETH_EN信号配置为逻辑低关闭VCC3V3_ETH电源输出。 3、 运行模式 TPS22918正常输出VCC3V3_ETH。 Power on mode The MCU is used to power the MCU ETH system by configuring the MCU_3V3_ETH_EN signal as a logical high enabled VCC3V3_ETH power output. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | ID：SWRS_TC3XX_030; SM Name：SMC[SW]:MCU:LBIST_CFG; SM TSR：In initialization process, the SW shall configure LBIST parameters via LBISTCTRL0, LBISTCTRL1, LBISTCTRL2 and start LBIST execution. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | 3.3.5 EQ6最小系统 EQ6 minimum system 553.3.6 摄像头最小系统 camera minimum system 593.3.7 看门狗电路 Watchdog circuit 613.3.8 温度采样电路 Temp Sample circuit 623.3.9 ETH电路 ETH circuit 633.3.10 CAN FD电路 CAN FD circuit 654 ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition threshold 685 EQ6L上下电时序要求 EQ6L Power-on and power-off sequence requirements 686 共享和独占的硬件资源 Shared and Exclusive Hardware Resource 696.1 ECU-MCU信号对应关系 ECU-MCU Signal Mapping 696.2 MCU时钟配置 MCU Clock Configuration 706.3 存储器配置 Memeory Configuration 717 其他芯片配置 Other IC Configuration 717.1 其他芯片的通讯机制 Communication Mechanism with Other IC 717.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration 727.3 TPS62872配置 TPS62872 Configuration 728 安全机制 Safety Mechanisms 736.1 System level 736.2 HW level 736.3 IC level 73概述 Introduction目的 Purpose软硬接口文档的目的是描述软件和硬件的交互，并且确保软件硬件和TSR的一致性。The goal of the hardware software interface document is to specify the hardware and software interaction and be consistent with the technical safety concept.适用范围 Range of Application此文档适用于奇瑞EQ6L T1G ADAS项目的功能安全开发全过程。This document is applied for the whole functional safety development process of EQ6L T1G ADAS project.读者对象 TReader项目经理Project Manager项目组成员Project Team Members功能安全经理Functional Safety Manager功能安全工程师Functional Safety System Engineer硬件工程师 Hardware Engineer软件工程师 Software Engineer质量和审查人员QA and Assessor产品测试人员Product Test Engineer

问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：根据图纸引脚映射，U1800的输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络如下： - **输入网络**：VCC3V3（连接至VIN引脚） - **输出检测网络**：VOSNS连接至VCC0V75（输出电压检测），GOSNS连接至GND（地检测） - **使能网络**：N19888813（连接至EN引脚） - **I²C控制网络**：SDA连接至MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL连接至MCU_SCL_P15.4 **证据来源**： - `circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中列出的引脚映射：VIN→VCC3V3，VOSNS→VCC0V75，GOSNS→GND，EN→N19888813，SDA→MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL→MCU_SCL_P15.4。
评分正文：根据图纸引脚映射，U1800的输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络如下： - **输入网络**：VCC3V3（连接至VIN引脚） - **输出检测网络**：VOSNS连接至VCC0V75（输出电压检测），GOSNS连接至GND（地检测） - **使能网络**：N19888813（连接至EN引脚） - **I²C控制网络**：SDA连接至MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL连接至MCU_SCL_P15.4 **证据来源**： - `circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中列出的引脚映射：VIN→VCC3V3，VOSNS→VCC0V75，GOSNS→GND，EN→N19888813，SDA→MCU_SDA_P15.5，SCL→MCU_SCL_P15.4。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. MCU的ADC采集到UBD后进行电源管理，当软件诊断到UBD电压范围异常时，MCU会关断EQ和其他负载工作电源，确保ADAS功能安全。 The ADC of MCU collects UBD for power management. 问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：**结论：** VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过的负载开关是 **TPS22918**，其使能信号为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**（由 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚控制）。 **证据支撑：** 1. **负载开关型号** - 文档[18]（F19 模块方案）明确说明：“采用 TPS22918 负载开关，将 VCC1V8 电源转换为 VCC1V8_EQ，给 EQ6 供电”。 - 文档[20]（F19 模块列表）记载：“TPS22918 负载开关，VCC1V8电压输入，VCC1V8_EQ电压输出”。 - 电路图[9][12]中，网络 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN 连接到 U1803（负载开关）的 ON 引脚，网络 VCC1V8_EQ 连接到 U1803 的 VOUT 引脚，印证了该负载开关的存在。 2. **使能信号** - 文档[18]说明：“用 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚使能该芯片”。 - 电路图[9]显示网络 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN 连接至 U900E（MCU）的 P20.0 引脚，同时连接至 U1803 的 ON 引脚（即使能引脚）。 - 文档[17]（模块功能描述）也指出：“VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ … 用 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚使能该芯片”。 **缺失信息：** 无。所有必要信息均已覆盖。
评分正文：**结论：** VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过的负载开关是 **TPS22918**，其使能信号为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**（由 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚控制）。 **证据支撑：** 1. **负载开关型号** - 文档[18]（F19 模块方案）明确说明：“采用 TPS22918 负载开关，将 VCC1V8 电源转换为 VCC1V8_EQ，给 EQ6 供电”。 - 文档[20]（F19 模块列表）记载：“TPS22918 负载开关，VCC1V8电压输入，VCC1V8_EQ电压输出”。 - 电路图[9][12]中，网络 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN 连接到 U1803（负载开关）的 ON 引脚，网络 VCC1V8_EQ 连接到 U1803 的 VOUT 引脚，印证了该负载开关的存在。 2. **使能信号** - 文档[18]说明：“用 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚使能该芯片”。 - 电路图[9]显示网络 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN 连接至 U900E（MCU）的 P20.0 引脚，同时连接至 U1803 的 ON 引脚（即使能引脚）。 - 文档[17]（模块功能描述）也指出：“VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ … 用 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚使能该芯片”。 **缺失信息：** 无。所有必要信息均已覆盖。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. .1 5 Description (continued) The switching frequency is resistor-selectable through the FSEL pin. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. The user cannot select the start-up voltage and I2C address independently.(1) The user can use the VSEL pin to select which of the four start-up voltages the device uses. If used outside the Recommended Operating Conditions but within the Absolute Maximum Ratings, the device may not be fully functional, and this may affect device reliability, functionality, performance, and shorten the device lifetime. Current Limit (Hiccup) INCORRECT CORRECT TPS6287x-Q1 TPS6287x-Q1 2.7 V to 6 V 2.7Vto6 V VIN ≥ 15 kQ EN INCORRECT CORRECT TPS6287x-Q1 TPS6287x-Q1 2.7V to 6 V 2.7V to 6 V VIN ≥15 kΩ ENABLE ENABLE EN | 9.3.11 Undervoltage Lockout (UVLO) The device has an undervoltage lockout function that disables the device if the supply voltage is too low for correct operation. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation

问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：**直接结论：** 文档声明板上有4个CAN相关模块：CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3。网表可确认其具体连接，包括差分信号线、待机控制、休眠控制等网络。 **支撑证据：** 1. **文档声明的CAN相关模块**（来源：`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）： - **CAN0**：外挂TCAN1145收发器，连接至ECU Connector X1900-2/X1900-3（CAN0H/CAN0L），MCU引脚P13.0（TXD）、P13.1（RXD），待机控制SPI3片选（P02.6）[20]。 - **CAN1**：外挂TCAN1044A收发器，连接至X1900-10/X1900-11（CAN1H/CAN1L），MCU引脚P20.7（TXD）、P20.6（RXD），待机控制SPI3片选（P00.2）[18]。 - **CAN2**：外挂TCAN1044A收发器，连接至X1900-4/X1900-12（CAN2H/CAN2L），MCU引脚P14.0（TXD）、P14.1（RXD），待机控制SPI3片选（P00.5）[17]。 - **CAN3**：外挂TCAN1044A收发器，连接至X1900-16/X1900-15（CAN3H/CAN3L），MCU引脚P32.3（TXD）、P32.2（RXD），待机控制SPI3片选（P00.8）[19]。 2. **网表确认的具体连接**（来源：`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）： - **CAN0模块**： - CAN0H：连接至C1201.&1, D1200.cathode1, L1200.&4, R1209.&2, R1214.&2, TP1206.&1, X1900.&2 [1]。 - CAN0L：连接至C1200.&1, D1200.cathode2, L1200.&3, R1212.&2, R1215.&2, TP1215.&1, X1900.&3 [2]。 - CAN0_INH：连接至D1203.&1, R1630.&1, TP1219.&1, U1200.INH, U1203.INH [3]。 - CAN0_INH_ACQ：连接至C1216.&1, R1236.&2, R1237.&2, TP1232.&1, U900A.AN20/EVADC_G2CH4/EDSADC_EDS2PA [4]。 - CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8：连接至R1126.&1, R1228.&2, R1241.&1, TP1217.&1, U1200.SCSN, U1202.STB, U1203.NSTB [5]。 - **CAN1模块**： - CAN1H：连接至C1207.&1, D1201.cathode1, L1201.&4, R1220.&2, R1224.&2, TP1222.&1, X1900.&10 [6]。 - CAN1L：连接至C1206.&1, D1201.cathode2, L1201.&3, R1223.&2, R1225.&2, TP1223.&1, X1900.&11 [7]。 - CAN1_INH：连接至D1204.&1, R1629.&1, TP1228.&1, U1204.INH, U1205.INH [8]。 - **CAN2模块**： - CAN2H：连接至C1301.&1, D1300.cathode1, L1300.&4, R1302.&2, R1306.&2, TP1301.&1, X1900.&4 [9]。 - CAN2L：连接至C1300.&1, D1300.cathode2, L1300.&3, R1305.&2, R1307.&2, TP1303.&1, X1900.&12 [10]。 - **CAN3模块**： - CAN3H：连接至C1306.&1, D1301.cathode1, L1301.&4, R1311.&2, R1315.&2, TP1307.&1, X1900.&16 [11]。 - CAN3L：连接至C1305.&1, D1301.cathode2, L1301.&3, R1314.&2, R1316.&2, TP1309.&1, X1900.&15 [12]。 **结论：** 文档明确声明了CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3四个模块，网表提供了每个模块的差分信号线、休眠控制、待机控制等网络的详细连接器件，可相互验证。
评分正文：**直接结论：** 文档声明板上有4个CAN相关模块：CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3。网表可确认其具体连接，包括差分信号线、待机控制、休眠控制等网络。 **支撑证据：** 1. **文档声明的CAN相关模块**（来源：`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）： - **CAN0**：外挂TCAN1145收发器，连接至ECU Connector X1900-2/X1900-3（CAN0H/CAN0L），MCU引脚P13.0（TXD）、P13.1（RXD），待机控制SPI3片选（P02.6）[20]。 - **CAN1**：外挂TCAN1044A收发器，连接至X1900-10/X1900-11（CAN1H/CAN1L），MCU引脚P20.7（TXD）、P20.6（RXD），待机控制SPI3片选（P00.2）[18]。 - **CAN2**：外挂TCAN1044A收发器，连接至X1900-4/X1900-12（CAN2H/CAN2L），MCU引脚P14.0（TXD）、P14.1（RXD），待机控制SPI3片选（P00.5）[17]。 - **CAN3**：外挂TCAN1044A收发器，连接至X1900-16/X1900-15（CAN3H/CAN3L），MCU引脚P32.3（TXD）、P32.2（RXD），待机控制SPI3片选（P00.8）[19]。 2. **网表确认的具体连接**（来源：`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）： - **CAN0模块**： - CAN0H：连接至C1201.&1, D1200.cathode1, L1200.&4, R1209.&2, R1214.&2, TP1206.&1, X1900.&2 [1]。 - CAN0L：连接至C1200.&1, D1200.cathode2, L1200.&3, R1212.&2, R1215.&2, TP1215.&1, X1900.&3 [2]。 - CAN0_INH：连接至D1203.&1, R1630.&1, TP1219.&1, U1200.INH, U1203.INH [3]。 - CAN0_INH_ACQ：连接至C1216.&1, R1236.&2, R1237.&2, TP1232.&1, U900A.AN20/EVADC_G2CH4/EDSADC_EDS2PA [4]。 - CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8：连接至R1126.&1, R1228.&2, R1241.&1, TP1217.&1, U1200.SCSN, U1202.STB, U1203.NSTB [5]。 - **CAN1模块**： - CAN1H：连接至C1207.&1, D1201.cathode1, L1201.&4, R1220.&2, R1224.&2, TP1222.&1, X1900.&10 [6]。 - CAN1L：连接至C1206.&1, D1201.cathode2, L1201.&3, R1223.&2, R1225.&2, TP1223.&1, X1900.&11 [7]。 - CAN1_INH：连接至D1204.&1, R1629.&1, TP1228.&1, U1204.INH, U1205.INH [8]。 - **CAN2模块**： - CAN2H：连接至C1301.&1, D1300.cathode1, L1300.&4, R1302.&2, R1306.&2, TP1301.&1, X1900.&4 [9]。 - CAN2L：连接至C1300.&1, D1300.cathode2, L1300.&3, R1305.&2, R1307.&2, TP1303.&1, X1900.&12 [10]。 - **CAN3模块**： - CAN3H：连接至C1306.&1, D1301.cathode1, L1301.&4, R1311.&2, R1315.&2, TP1307.&1, X1900.&16 [11]。 - CAN3L：连接至C1305.&1, D1301.cathode2, L1301.&3, R1314.&2, R1316.&2, TP1309.&1, X1900.&15 [12]。 **结论：** 文档明确声明了CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3四个模块，网表提供了每个模块的差分信号线、休眠控制、待机控制等网络的详细连接器件，可相互验证。

NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F20/F21/F22/F23</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>运行模式、待机模式、Sleep模式、OFF模式、Overtemp模式 Run mode, standby mode, Sleep mode, OFF mode, Overtemp mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode
