Reset the chip with P12.1 pin of the MCU. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | 10.3 Best Design Practices Figure 10-22.Shutdown (Discharge Enabled) 10.2.3 Application Curves (continued) 100MS/s 250MS/'s 3.30 3.30 V →3.0000ms T+600.000s Load = 0.75 Ω Load = 7.5 Q Figure 10-20. 问题：不要概括，逐颗列出所有 TPS22918 的输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制。
参考答案：应逐颗列出 U1500、U1802、U1803，分别对应 VCC3V3→VCC3V3_ETH、VCC3V3→VCC3V3_EQ、VCC1V8→VCC1V8_EQ，并给出三个 MCU 使能网络。
实际回答：**直接结论：** 电路中存在三颗 TPS22918 器件，分别为 U1500、U1802 和 U1803。它们的输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制引脚连接如下： - **U1500**：输入电源（VIN）→ VCC3V3，输出电源（VOUT）→ VCC3V3_ETH，ON 控制 → MCU_3V3_ETH_EN。 - **U1802**：输入电源（VIN）→ VCC3V3，输出电源（VOUT）→ VCC3V3_EQ，ON 控制 → MCU_3V3_EQ_EN。 - **U1803**：输入电源（VIN）→ VCC1V8，输出电源（VOUT）→ VCC1V8_EQ，ON 控制 → MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。 **支撑证据：** - 证据 [1]（引脚映射 U1803）：VIN → VCC1V8，VOUT → VCC1V8_EQ，ON → MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。 - 证据 [2]（引脚映射 U1500）：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_ETH，ON → MCU_3V3_ETH_EN。 - 证据 [3]（引脚映射 U1802）：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_EQ，ON → MCU_3V3_EQ_EN。 - 证据 [12]、[13]、[14]（实例信息）确认 U1500、U1802、U1803 的器件型号为 TPS22918。 **缺失信息：** 无。
评分正文：**直接结论：** 电路中存在三颗 TPS22918 器件，分别为 U1500、U1802 和 U1803。它们的输入电源、输出电源和 ON 控制引脚连接如下： - **U1500**：输入电源（VIN）→ VCC3V3，输出电源（VOUT）→ VCC3V3_ETH，ON 控制 → MCU_3V3_ETH_EN。 - **U1802**：输入电源（VIN）→ VCC3V3，输出电源（VOUT）→ VCC3V3_EQ，ON 控制 → MCU_3V3_EQ_EN。 - **U1803**：输入电源（VIN）→ VCC1V8，输出电源（VOUT）→ VCC1V8_EQ，ON 控制 → MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。 **支撑证据：** - 证据 [1]（引脚映射 U1803）：VIN → VCC1V8，VOUT → VCC1V8_EQ，ON → MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。 - 证据 [2]（引脚映射 U1500）：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_ETH，ON → MCU_3V3_ETH_EN。 - 证据 [3]（引脚映射 U1802）：VIN → VCC3V3，VOUT → VCC3V3_EQ，ON → MCU_3V3_EQ_EN。 - 证据 [12]、[13]、[14]（实例信息）确认 U1500、U1802、U1803 的器件型号为 TPS22918。 **缺失信息：** 无。

hw-v1-circuit-vcc3v3-rolesuccess0.2660.4211.000not_applicable0.5001.0001.0000.625not_applicable
问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：根据现有证据，VCC3V3网络在板上主要供电给以下电源器件： - **U1500** (TPS22918 负载开关)：证据[8]和[16]显示，VCC3V3通过U1500转换为VCC3V3_ETH，用于ETH供电。证据[1]中U1500的VIN引脚直接连接VCC3V3。 - **U1802** (TPS22918 负载开关)：证据[8]和[16]显示，VCC3V3通过U1802转换为VCC3V3_EQ，用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电。证据[1]中U1802的VIN引脚直接连接VCC3V3。 - **U1501**：证据[5]显示U1501的IN引脚连接VCC3V3_ETH，但其输入来源为VCC3V3（通过U1500转换），因此其供电依赖VCC3V3。证据[8]中U1501被描述为“电源转换芯片TPS62872，VCC3V3转VCC0V75”，但未明确其输入网络直接来自VCC3V3主网络。 *（缺口：U1501的输入网络是否直接为VCC3V3，证据不充分，仅从子网络VCC3V3_ETH间接推断。）* - **U901**：证据[7]显示U901的S2引脚连接VCC3V3_SW子网络，该子网络由VCC3V3通过Q1700转换而来，但U901的具体型号和功能未在证据中明确。 *（缺口：U901的器件类型和功能描述缺失，无法确认其为电源器件。）* **缺口说明**：以下子问题未完全覆盖： - **从VCC3V3网络连接的器件中识别出所有电源器件**：无法从给定证据中完整识别出VCC3V3连接的所有IC类器件的型号和功能，尤其是U1600、U1700、U1800、U1804等器件的具体类型未在证据中描述，因此无法确认它们是否为电源器件。
评分正文：根据现有证据，VCC3V3网络在板上主要供电给以下电源器件： - **U1500** (TPS22918 负载开关)：证据[8]和[16]显示，VCC3V3通过U1500转换为VCC3V3_ETH，用于ETH供电。证据[1]中U1500的VIN引脚直接连接VCC3V3。 - **U1802** (TPS22918 负载开关)：证据[8]和[16]显示，VCC3V3通过U1802转换为VCC3V3_EQ，用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电。证据[1]中U1802的VIN引脚直接连接VCC3V3。 - **U1501**：证据[5]显示U1501的IN引脚连接VCC3V3_ETH，但其输入来源为VCC3V3（通过U1500转换），因此其供电依赖VCC3V3。证据[8]中U1501被描述为“电源转换芯片TPS62872，VCC3V3转VCC0V75”，但未明确其输入网络直接来自VCC3V3主网络。 *（缺口：U1501的输入网络是否直接为VCC3V3，证据不充分，仅从子网络VCC3V3_ETH间接推断。）* - **U901**：证据[7]显示U901的S2引脚连接VCC3V3_SW子网络，该子网络由VCC3V3通过Q1700转换而来，但U901的具体型号和功能未在证据中明确。 *（缺口：U901的器件类型和功能描述缺失，无法确认其为电源器件。）* **缺口说明**：以下子问题未完全覆盖： - **从VCC3V3网络连接的器件中识别出所有电源器件**：无法从给定证据中完整识别出VCC3V3连接的所有IC类器件的型号和功能，尤其是U1600、U1700、U1800、U1804等器件的具体类型未在证据中描述，因此无法确认它们是否为电源器件。

ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F32</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>3V3转1V25_MCU用于MCU的核供电 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is used for the core power supply of MCU</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>该BUCK电源使用MCU的内置控制器进行控制，外部需要选型功率开关管和电感电容。 The BUCK power supply is controlled by the built-in controller of the MCU, and the external selection of power switches and inductors and capacitors is required.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F19</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>1V8转1V8_EQ用于EQ6供电，现选择负载开关方案。 1V8 to 1V8_EQ is used for the EQ6 power supply, now choose the load switch scheme. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ，给EQ6供电。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch chip is used to convert the VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ to power EQ6. When the software detects that UBD voltage range is abnormal, the MCU will turn off EQ and other load power supplies to ensure the functional safety of ADAS.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F14</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。 VCC3V3 to VCCOV75 is applied to the kernel power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. TI's scheme TPS62872 is selected.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 采用TPS62872电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75，提供给EQ6核电供电。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 TPS62872 power chip is used to convert VCC3V3 to VCCOV75, which is supplied to EQ nuclear power supply. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable chip, MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set output voltage and communication. +</td></tr></table> | 问题：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节？
参考答案：应查 HSI 7.2 节“LP87702 配置”，其中给出软件寄存器和 I²C 配置说明。
实际回答：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的第 **7.2 节“LP87702 配置”**。 **支撑证据：** - 证据[2]（HSI 文档正文）明确列出“LP87702 配置 LP87702 Configuration”小节，并说明“LP87702 运行期间需要 MCU 通过 I2C 配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”。 - 证据[3]（HSI 文档目录）显示“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”位于第 72 页。 **缺失信息：** 无。
评分正文：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的第 **7.2 节“LP87702 配置”**。 **支撑证据：** - 证据[2]（HSI 文档正文）明确列出“LP87702 配置 LP87702 Configuration”小节，并说明“LP87702 运行期间需要 MCU 通过 I2C 配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”。 - 证据[3]（HSI 文档目录）显示“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”位于第 72 页。 **缺失信息：** 无。

READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. 问题：网表写 TC367、接口文档写 TC377，这两个说法怎么处理？
参考答案：必须保留来源差异：EDF 的 U900 型号是 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，而 HSI 章节名是 TC377 最小系统；现有证据不足以把二者无条件视为同一准确型号。
实际回答：根据现有证据，网表写TC367、接口文档写TC377，这两个说法是**不一致的**，且**没有直接证据**表明它们之间的关系或变更原因。 **1. 网表中TC367的上下文** - **证据**：电路设计网表（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中，实例U900A和U900B的型号均为`SAK-TC367DP-64F300S AA`，且存在多个以`TC367_`命名的网络（如`TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_RESET_N`等），这些网络连接至MCU的JTAG和复位引脚。 - **结论**：网表明确指向型号为**TC367**的MCU，并定义了其相关引脚连接。 **2. 接口文档中TC377的上下文** - **证据**：接口文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）的模块定义表（F01）和章节标题中，MCU型号明确写为`SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`，该文档整体描述的是基于**TC377**的最小系统。 - **结论**：接口文档明确指向型号为**TC377**的MCU，并围绕其进行系统设计描述。 **3. 是否有官方变更记录或勘误表说明型号变更** - **证据**：在提供的所有电路设计文件和接口文档中，**均未发现**任何官方变更记录、勘误表或说明，来解释型号从TC367改为TC377或反之。 - **缺口**：**缺失**。 **4. 两个型号的实际规格差异及是否可互换** - **证据**：接口文档中提及TC377的功能安全等级可达ASIL-D，但**未提供**TC367的规格参数、封装、内存等直接对比信息。网表中TC367的封装为`bga80p20x20_1700x1700x170-292`，但TC377的封装信息在证据中缺失。 - **缺口**：**缺失**。现有证据不足以判断两个型号的规格差异和互换性。
评分正文：根据现有证据，网表写TC367、接口文档写TC377，这两个说法是**不一致的**，且**没有直接证据**表明它们之间的关系或变更原因。 **1. 网表中TC367的上下文** - **证据**：电路设计网表（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中，实例U900A和U900B的型号均为`SAK-TC367DP-64F300S AA`，且存在多个以`TC367_`命名的网络（如`TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_RESET_N`等），这些网络连接至MCU的JTAG和复位引脚。 - **结论**：网表明确指向型号为**TC367**的MCU，并定义了其相关引脚连接。 **2. 接口文档中TC377的上下文** - **证据**：接口文档（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）的模块定义表（F01）和章节标题中，MCU型号明确写为`SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`，该文档整体描述的是基于**TC377**的最小系统。 - **结论**：接口文档明确指向型号为**TC377**的MCU，并围绕其进行系统设计描述。 **3. 是否有官方变更记录或勘误表说明型号变更** - **证据**：在提供的所有电路设计文件和接口文档中，**均未发现**任何官方变更记录、勘误表或说明，来解释型号从TC367改为TC377或反之。 - **缺口**：**缺失**。 **4. 两个型号的实际规格差异及是否可互换** - **证据**：接口文档中提及TC377的功能安全等级可达ASIL-D，但**未提供**TC367的规格参数、封装、内存等直接对比信息。网表中TC367的封装为`bga80p20x20_1700x1700x170-292`，但TC377的封装信息在证据中缺失。 - **缺口**：**缺失**。现有证据不足以判断两个型号的规格差异和互换性。

resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | 模板变更历史Template Change History：1; None：NA; None：首次发布; None：首次发布; None：01; None：2021-08-26 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：王会苹 ——模板变更历史Template change history 模板变更历史Template Change History：2; None：HR-CIMA-CI-DCR_202210002; None：模板优化调整; None：1. 添加填写说明页与定义页 2. 封面内容修订，字体字号、底色布局等格式优化; None：02; None：2023-06-10 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：王会苹 ——模板变更历史Template change history 模板变更历史Template Change History：3; None：HR-CIMA-CI-TCR_202312002; None：模板中需增加复用说明; None：增加HSI复用说明列; None：03; None：2023-10-10 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：王会苹 ——模板变更历史Template change history 模板变更历史Template Change History：4; None：HR-CIMA-CI-TCR_202401001; None：功能安全相关模板修订和其他模板修订; None：新增“软件需使能低压复位功能，低压阈值与硬件工程师沟通确认”要求; None：04; None：2024-01-24 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：王会苹 ——模板变更历史Template change history 模板变更历史Template Change History：5; None：HR-CIMA-CI-TCR_202409001; None：一个硬件的接口可能会对应多个软件功能; None：1.增加基于系统开发的角度写的HIS example4 2.更新HSI模板; None：05; None：2024-09-10 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：师明 ——模板变更历史Template change history | 顾客代表Customer Representative其他与项目相关人员等Other relevant person术语及缩写解释 Terms and AbbreviationsTable 1-1Terms and Abbreviations参考文档 Reference DocumentTable 1-2Reference Document硬件架构 Hardware Architecture硬件架构描述了60060894_ADAS项目的硬件基础模块，模块和模块之间的接口。The Hardware Architecture describe the HW basic component of 600608964_ADAS project, as well as the interface of each component.系统概述 System overviewADAS产品要求功能安全达到ASIL-B，硬件整体架构介绍如下：ADAS products require functional safety to meet ASIL-B. The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、上电模式 MCU使能EQ6系统电源，EQ6上电后将VCC3V3_EQ和VCC1V8通过FPC连接器提供给摄像头，其中VCC3V3_EQ再转换成VCC1V1，EQ6运行后配置RESET_N为高电平，并通过I2C配置CAM参数，摄像头开始工作。 2、运行模式 摄像头正常运行，输出图像信息给EQ6。 3、复位模式 EQ6配置摄像头的RESET_N信号为低电平，CAM进入复位模式。 4、下电模式 MCU控制EQ6系统下电（VCC3V3_EQ、VCC1V8）。 Power on mode MCU enables EQ6 system power supply, After EQ6 is powered on, VCC3V3_EQ and VCCIV8 are provided to the camera through the FPC connector, where VCC3V3_EQ is converted into VCC1V1. After EQ6 runs, RESET_N is configured to high level, and CAM parameters are configured through I2C, and the camera starts to work. Run mode The camera operates normally and outputs the image information to EQ6. Reset mode EQ6 config the RESET_N signal of the camera to be low and the CAM enters the reset mode. Power off mode MCU controls the power down of EQ6 system (VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8).</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>摄像头上需要3路电源（VCC3V3、VCC1V8、VCC1V1），其中VCC1V8和VCC3V3是主板提供，VCC1V1由3V3转换得到。 摄像头复位信号由EQ6通过GPIO口进行控制。 摄像头3路电源的上下电和复位信号的时序要求如下所示： The camera needs 3 power sources (VCC2V8, VCC1V8, VCC1V05), VCC1V8 and VCC3V3 are provided by the Main board, VCC1V1 is converted from 3V3. hw-v1-joint-pmic-noisy-querysuccess0.4550.4140.857not_applicable0.1430.6000.5000.571not_applicable
问题：忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容，只结合电路与 HSI 说明 LP87702 的供电输出和配置。
参考答案：LP87702-Q1 是 U1700，输入 VCC3V3，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50；配置依据 HSI 7.2 节，回答不应混入无关摄像头或 CAN 结论。
实际回答：### 供电输出参数（sq_1，部分覆盖） **直接结论**：LP87702 的输入为 VCC3V3，输出三路电源：**VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50**。VCC1V8 用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 供电；VCC1V1 用于 EQ6、DDR 供电；VCC50 用于 CAN 收发器供电。 **支撑证据**： - 证据 [7]（HSI 文档模块方案）明确说明：“VCC1V8 用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 供电，VCC1V1 用于 EQ6、DDR 供电，VCC50 用于 CAN 收发器 供电，现选择 LP87702 PMIC 方案。” - 证据 [9]（HSI 文档模块功能）同样描述：“LP87702 chip is used to convert VCC3V3 into VCC1V8, VCC1V1 and VCC50”。 - 证据 [10]（HSI 文档模块列表）和证据 [11]（模块列表）均确认该输入输出关系。 **缺口说明**：**缺少各输出引脚的电流能力参数，以及具体的引脚编号定义**（仅知道使能引脚 P15.3 控制 5V 输出，I2C 引脚为 MCU_SCL_P15.4 和 MCU_SDA_P15.5，复位引脚 P12.1）。电路设计文档（证据 [1]）仅提供了器件型号 U1700: LP87702-Q1，未包含任何引脚网络或电压电流参数。 --- ### 配置寄存器与模式设置（sq_2，已覆盖） **直接结论**：LP87702 支持 **SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE** 四种运行模式。MCU 通过 I2C（地址 **0x08**）设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 输出电压及使能，并通过 P15.3 引脚使能 5V 输出，P12.1 引脚复位。具体的配置寄存器表在 HSI 文档中提及但未提供完整内容，已知的部分寄存器位描述包含过压/欠压反应设置（如 FS_I_OVUV_SAFE_REACTION1）。 **支撑证据**： - 证据 [2]（HSI 文档模式定义）列出了四种运行模式及其定义，包括 SHUTDOWN（NRST 低于阈值，器件停止）、READ OTP（上电加载 OTP）、STANDBY（电压转换器禁用，寄存器可读写）、ACTIVE（DC/DC 使能）。 - 证据 [7] 和 [9] 说明配置方式：用 MCU 的 P15.3 引脚使能 5V 输出，用 MCU_SCL_P15.4、MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C 通信设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 输出电压和使能，用 P12.1 引脚复位。 - 证据 [16]（HSI 文档接口信号）给出 I2C 地址 0x08，并确认 I2C 引脚用于配置 LP87702。 - 证据 [6]（HSI 文档寄存器片段）包含 LP87702 的寄存器位描述（如 VMON_LDO1_OV_FS_REACTION、VMON_LDO1_UV_FS_REACTION 等），说明寄存器配置涉及过欠压反应设置。注意：该证据未显示完整寄存器表，但已确认配置寄存器的存在。 **说明**：具体寄存器配置值（如 Table 5-2 的内容）在证据中未全文呈现，但配置方式、模式定义及部分寄存器位描述已足够支撑配置信息的提取。
评分正文：### 供电输出参数（sq_1，部分覆盖） **直接结论**：LP87702 的输入为 VCC3V3，输出三路电源：**VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50**。VCC1V8 用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 供电；VCC1V1 用于 EQ6、DDR 供电；VCC50 用于 CAN 收发器供电。 **支撑证据**： - 证据 [7]（HSI 文档模块方案）明确说明：“VCC1V8 用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 供电，VCC1V1 用于 EQ6、DDR 供电，VCC50 用于 CAN 收发器 供电，现选择 LP87702 PMIC 方案。” - 证据 [9]（HSI 文档模块功能）同样描述：“LP87702 chip is used to convert VCC3V3 into VCC1V8, VCC1V1 and VCC50”。 - 证据 [10]（HSI 文档模块列表）和证据 [11]（模块列表）均确认该输入输出关系。 **缺口说明**：**缺少各输出引脚的电流能力参数，以及具体的引脚编号定义**（仅知道使能引脚 P15.3 控制 5V 输出，I2C 引脚为 MCU_SCL_P15.4 和 MCU_SDA_P15.5，复位引脚 P12.1）。电路设计文档（证据 [1]）仅提供了器件型号 U1700: LP87702-Q1，未包含任何引脚网络或电压电流参数。 --- ### 配置寄存器与模式设置（sq_2，已覆盖） **直接结论**：LP87702 支持 **SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE** 四种运行模式。MCU 通过 I2C（地址 **0x08**）设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 输出电压及使能，并通过 P15.3 引脚使能 5V 输出，P12.1 引脚复位。具体的配置寄存器表在 HSI 文档中提及但未提供完整内容，已知的部分寄存器位描述包含过压/欠压反应设置（如 FS_I_OVUV_SAFE_REACTION1）。 **支撑证据**： - 证据 [2]（HSI 文档模式定义）列出了四种运行模式及其定义，包括 SHUTDOWN（NRST 低于阈值，器件停止）、READ OTP（上电加载 OTP）、STANDBY（电压转换器禁用，寄存器可读写）、ACTIVE（DC/DC 使能）。 - 证据 [7] 和 [9] 说明配置方式：用 MCU 的 P15.3 引脚使能 5V 输出，用 MCU_SCL_P15.4、MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C 通信设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 输出电压和使能，用 P12.1 引脚复位。 - 证据 [16]（HSI 文档接口信号）给出 I2C 地址 0x08，并确认 I2C 引脚用于配置 LP87702。 - 证据 [6]（HSI 文档寄存器片段）包含 LP87702 的寄存器位描述（如 VMON_LDO1_OV_FS_REACTION、VMON_LDO1_UV_FS_REACTION 等），说明寄存器配置涉及过欠压反应设置。注意：该证据未显示完整寄存器表，但已确认配置寄存器的存在。 **说明**：具体寄存器配置值（如 Table 5-2 的内容）在证据中未全文呈现，但配置方式、模式定义及部分寄存器位描述已足够支撑配置信息的提取。

STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. This register returns the device status flags. 10 Application and Implementation Note Information in the following applications sections is not part of the Tl component specification, and Tl does not warrant its accuracy or completeness. Tl's customers are responsible for determining suitability of components for their purposes, as well as validating and testing their design implementation to confirm systemfunctionality. 10.1 Application Information The following section discusses selection of the external components to complete the power supply design for typical a application. 10.2 Typical Application 10.2.1 Design Requirements Preliminary Calculations The maximum allowable deviation of the power supply is ±3.3%. The DC accuracy of the TPS6287x-Q1 is specified as ±1%, therefore, the maximum output voltage variation during a transient is given by: VoUT = ± VouT × (3.3% - 1%) = ±17.25 mV 10.2.2 Detailed Design Procedure The following subsections describe how to calculate the external components required to meet the specified transient requirements of a given application. The calculations include the worst-case variation of components and use the RMS method to combine the variation of uncorrelated parameters. 10.2.2.1 Selecting the Inductor The TPS6287x-Q1 devices have been optimized for inductors in the range 50 nH to 300 nH. If the transient response of the converter is limited by the slew rate of the current in the inductor, using a smaller inductor can improve performance. However, the output ripple current increases as the value of the inductor decreases and higher output current ripple generates higher output voltage ripple, which adds to the transient overshoot or undershoot. The optimal configuration for a given application is always a trade-off between a number of parameters. Tl recommends a starting value of 110 nH for typical applications. | MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：E11; None：P12.1; None：P12.1/GTM_TIM4_IN1_6/GTM_TIM3_IN1_6/GETH_MDIOC/GTM_TOUT129/CAN00_TXD/GETH_MDIO; None：GPIO; None：MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1; None：/; None：I; None：LP87702的输出中断信号。 高：无中断信号 低：输出中断信号; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 HW Interface Component ：QM(B); None：F13/POWER F14/POWER; None：MCU配置LP87702、TPS62872; MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：E14; None：P15.5; None：P15.5/GTM_TIM3_IN0_4/GTM_TIM2_IN0_4/QSPI2_MTSRA/GTM_TOUT76/QSPI2_MTSR/MSC0_EN0/I2C0_SDA; None：I2C0_SDAC; None：MCU_SDA_P15.5; None：/; None：IO; None：IIC_SDA信号 外挂LP87702和TPS62872 LP87702地址：0x08 TPS62872地址：0x41; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：A17; None：P15.4; None：P15.4/GTM_TIM3_IN7_4/GTM_TIM2_IN7_4/I2C0_SCLC/QSPI2_MRSTA/SCU_E_REQ0_0/GTM_TOUT75/QSPI2_MRST; None：I2C0_SCL; None：MCU_SCL_P15.4; None：/; None：O; None：IIC_CLK信号 外挂LP87702和TPS62872 LP87702地址：0x08 TPS62872地址：0x41; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | During start-up, the output voltage ramps up to the target value set by the VSEL pin before ramping up or down to any new value programmed to the device over the I2C interface. 9.3.6.3Non-DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoint If none of the default voltage range or voltage setpoint combinations are suitable for the application, the user can change these device settings through I2C before the user enables the device. Then, when the user pulls the EN pin high, the device starts up with the desired start-up voltage. Note that if the user changes the device settings through I?C while the device is ramping, the device ignores the changes until the ramp is complete 9.3.6.4 Dynamic Voltage Scaling If the user changes the output voltage setpoint while the DC/DC converter is operating, the device ramps up or down to the new voltage setting in a controlled way. The VRAMP[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register sets the slew rate when the device ramps from one voltage to another during DVS (see Table 9-6). Note that ramping the output to a higher voltage requires additional output current, so that during DvS, the converter must generate a total output current given by: dVour lour = lour c) + Cour dVOouT where · lout is the total current the converter must generate while ramping to a higher voltage. louT(Dc) is the DC load current.· Cout is the total output capacitance. dVout/dt is the slew rate of the output voltage (programmable in the range 0.5 mV/μs to 10 mV/us). For correct operation, ensure that the total output current during DVS does not exceed the current limit of the device. 9.3.7 Compensation (CoMP) The CoMP pin is the connection point for an external compensation network. A series-connected resistor and capacitor to GOSNS is sufficient for typical applications. The series-connected resistor also provides enough scope to optimize the loop response for a wide range of operating conditions. When using multiple devices in a stacked configuration, all devices share a common compensation network, and the COMP pin makes sure there is equal current sharing between them (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.8 ModeSelection and Clock Synchronization (MODE/SYNC) A high level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects forced PWM operation. A low level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects power save operation, in which, the device automatically transitions between PWM and PFM, according to the load conditions. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | Vin is the input voltage Vout is the output voltage.· · L is the effective inductance at the peak current level. tpd is the propagation delay of the current limit comparator (typically 50 ns). 9.3.13.2HiccupMode To enable hiccup operation, ensure that HICCUPEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. If hiccup operation is enabled and the high-side switch current exceeds the current limit threshold on 32 consecutive switching cycles, the device: Stops switching for 128 clock cycles, after which it automatically starts switching again (it starts a new soft-start sequence) · Sets the HICCUP bit in the STATUS register Pulls the PG pin low. The PG pin stays low until the overload condition goes away and the device can start up correctly and regulate the output voltage. Note that power-good function has a deglitch circuit, which delays the rising edge of the power-good signal by 40 μs (typical). Hiccup operation continues in a repeating sequence of 32 cycles in current limit, followed by a pause of 128 cycles, followed by a soft-start attempt for as long as the output overload condition exists. The device clears the HICCUP bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition has been removed. 9.3.13.3CurrentLimitMode To enable current limit mode, ensure that HICCUPEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. When current limit operation is enabled, the device limits the high-side switch current cycle-by-cycle for as long as the overload condition exists. If the device limits the high-side switch current for four or more consecutive switching cycles, the device sets ILIM = 1 in the STATUS register. The device clears the ILIM bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition no longer exits. 9.3.14 Power Good (PG) The power good (PG) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: · In a standalone configuration and in the primary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an open- drain output that indicates the status of the converter or stack. · In a secondary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an input that indicates when the soft-start sequence is complete and all converters in the stack can change from DCM switching to CCM switching. 9.3.14.1 Standalone orPrimaryDeviceBehavior The primary purpose of the PG pin is to indicate if the output voltage is in regulation, but it also indicates if the device is in thermal shutdown or disabled. Table 9-7 summarizes the behavior of the PG pin in a standalone or primary device. | If the user applies a valid clock signal to the MODE/SYNC pin, the device synchronizes its switching cycles to the external clock and automatically selects forced PwM operation. The MODE/SYNC pin is logically ORed with the FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Table 9-1). When multiple devices are used together in a stacked configuration, the MODE/SYNC pin of the secondary devices is the input for the clock signal (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.9 Spread Spectrum Clocking (SSC) The device has a spread spectrum clocking function that can reduce electromagnetic interference (EMl). When the SSC function is active, the device modulates the switching frequency to approximately ±10% the nominal value. The frequency modulation has a triangular characteristic (see Figure 9-11). To use the SSC function, make sure that: · SSCEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. · Forced PWM operation is selected (MODE pin is high or FPWMEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register). Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 ·The device is not synchronized to an external clock. To disable the SSC function, ensure that SCCEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. To use the SSC function with multiple devices in a stacked configuration, ensure that the primary converter runs from its internal oscillator and synchronize all secondary converters to the primary clock (see Figure 9-14). 9.3.10 Output Discharge The device has an output discharge function that ensures a defined ramp down of the output voltage when the device is disabled and keeps the output voltage close to 0 V while the device is off. The output discharge function is enabled when DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The output discharge function is enabled by default. If enabled, the device discharges the output under the following conditions: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = O in the CONTROL1 register. · A thermal shutdown event occurs. · A UVLO event occurs. · An OVLO event occurs. The output discharge function is not available until the user has enabled the device at least once after power up During power down, the device continues to discharge the output for as long as the internal supply voltage is greater than approximately 1.8 V. | If the junction temperature falls below the thermal shutdown threshold of 150°C (typical), the device: · Starts switching again, starting with a new soft-start sequence · Sets the EN pin to high impedance ·Sets the PG pin to high-impedance The device clears the TSHUT bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TSHUT threshold of 150°C (typical). In a stacked configuration, in which all devices share a common enable signal, a thermal shutdown condition in one device disables the entire stack. When the hot device cools down, the whole stack automatically starts switching again. 9.3.17 Stacked Operation The user can connect multiple devices in parallel in what is known as a "stack"; for example, to increase output current capability or reduce device junction temperature. A stack comprises one primary device and one or more secondary devices. During initialization, each device monitors its SYNC_OUT pin to determine if must operate as a primary device or a secondary device: · If there is a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground, the device operates as a secondary device. · If the SYNC_OUT pin is high impedance, the device operates as a primary device. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The key points to note are: All the devices in the stack share a common enable signal, which must be pulled up with a resistance of at least 15 kQ. All the devices in the stack share a common power-good signal. All the devices in the stack share a common compensation signal. All secondary devices must connect a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground. The remote sense pins (VOSNS and GOSNS) of each device must be connected (do not leave these pins floating). Each device must be configured for the same switching frequency.· The primary device must be configured for forced PwM operation (secondary devices are automatically configured for forcedPwM operation). A stacked configuration can support synchronization to an external clock or spread-spectrum clocking.· Only the VSEL pin of the primary device is used to set the default output voltage. The VSEL pin of secondary· devices is not used and must be connected to ground. The SDA and SCL pins of secondary devices are not used and must be connected to ground. | None：U20; None：P23.2; None：P23.2/GTM_TIM1_IN6_5/GTM_TIM0_IN6_5/GTM_TOUT43/CAN12_TXD; None：GPIO; None：MCU_RST_FLASH_P23.2; hw-v1-multihop-eth-enablesuccess0.6020.4500.571not_applicable0.0000.0001.0000.882not_applicable
问题：从 MCU 使能开始，追踪到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。
参考答案：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 使能 U1500，VCC3V3 经 U1500 形成 VCC3V3_ETH，再进入 U1501 TPS74501，输出 VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据电路设计文档，从MCU使能到以太网PHY 1.0V电源的完整路径存在两个关键缺失环节，无法建立完整追踪。 **直接结论**： 1. 未找到MCU使能信号直接连接到负责产生1.0V电源的LDO（U1501）的证据。 2. 未找到LDO（U1501）的1.0V输出（VCC1V0_ETH）连接到以太网PHY（U1400）电源引脚的证据。 **支撑证据**： - **LDO（U1501）的使能控制**：U1501的使能引脚（EN）连接至网络“VCC3V3_ETH”，而非MCU的任何使能信号。 来源：证据[1]（U1501引脚映射：EN -> VCC3V3_ETH）。 - **LDO（U1501）的1.0V输出**：U1501的输出引脚（OUT）连接至网络“VCC1V0_ETH”，该网络连接至电容C1420、C1506、C1507、电感L1403、电阻R1146、R1504、测试点TP1503。 来源：证据[18]（网络VCC1V0_ETH连接关系）。 - **以太网PHY（U1400）的电源引脚**：U1400的电源引脚VDDA、VDDIO、VDDMAC分别连接至网络“N22956977”和“N22956687”，未显示与网络“VCC1V0_ETH”有任何连接。 来源：证据[2]（U1400引脚映射：VDDA -> N22956977, VDDIO -> N22956687, VDDMAC -> N22956687）。 **缺失信息**： 1. **MCU使能信号到U1501的路径缺失**：电路设计文档中未发现任何MCU使能信号（如ECU_EN、CAN_EN等）连接到U1501的使能引脚（EN）或被U1501使用的直接证据。 2. **U1501输出到以太网PHY电源引脚的路径缺失**：无直接证据表明LDO输出网络“VCC1V0_ETH”与以太网PHY的电源引脚（VDDA、VDDIO、VDDMAC）相连。 **注意事项**： - 证据中提及的“VCC1V0_ETH”网络与以太网PHY电源网络“N22956977”或“N22956687”名称不同，无法基于相似性推断连接关系。 - 未发现任何LDO（U1501）或电源管理芯片直接输出1.0V至以太网PHY的电路连接。
评分正文：根据电路设计文档，从MCU使能到以太网PHY 1.0V电源的完整路径存在两个关键缺失环节，无法建立完整追踪。 **直接结论**： 1. 未找到MCU使能信号直接连接到负责产生1.0V电源的LDO（U1501）的证据。 2. 未找到LDO（U1501）的1.0V输出（VCC1V0_ETH）连接到以太网PHY（U1400）电源引脚的证据。 **支撑证据**： - **LDO（U1501）的使能控制**：U1501的使能引脚（EN）连接至网络“VCC3V3_ETH”，而非MCU的任何使能信号。 来源：证据[1]（U1501引脚映射：EN -> VCC3V3_ETH）。 - **LDO（U1501）的1.0V输出**：U1501的输出引脚（OUT）连接至网络“VCC1V0_ETH”，该网络连接至电容C1420、C1506、C1507、电感L1403、电阻R1146、R1504、测试点TP1503。 来源：证据[18]（网络VCC1V0_ETH连接关系）。 - **以太网PHY（U1400）的电源引脚**：U1400的电源引脚VDDA、VDDIO、VDDMAC分别连接至网络“N22956977”和“N22956687”，未显示与网络“VCC1V0_ETH”有任何连接。 来源：证据[2]（U1400引脚映射：VDDA -> N22956977, VDDIO -> N22956687, VDDMAC -> N22956687）。 **缺失信息**： 1. **MCU使能信号到U1501的路径缺失**：电路设计文档中未发现任何MCU使能信号（如ECU_EN、CAN_EN等）连接到U1501的使能引脚（EN）或被U1501使用的直接证据。 2. **U1501输出到以太网PHY电源引脚的路径缺失**：无直接证据表明LDO输出网络“VCC1V0_ETH”与以太网PHY的电源引脚（VDDA、VDDIO、VDDMAC）相连。 **注意事项**： - 证据中提及的“VCC1V0_ETH”网络与以太网PHY电源网络“N22956977”或“N22956687”名称不同，无法基于相似性推断连接关系。 - 未发现任何LDO（U1501）或电源管理芯片直接输出1.0V至以太网PHY的电路连接。
